    def body(self, ops):

        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)

buf49_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[6, 2304], stride=[2304, 1])
buf49_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[6, 2304], stride=[2304, 1])

buf50_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, 12, 6, 64], stride=[4608, 384, 64, 1])
class op50_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 6, p1: 12, p2: 64}
    index0 = p2
    index1 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + ((p2))
    index2 = p0 + 6*(ModularIndexing((p2), 1, 8))
    index3 = (p2)
    index4 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + (((p2))) + 8
    index5 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + ((((p2)) - 8))
    index6 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + ((p2 - 16)) + 16
    index7 = 64*p0 + 384*p1 + p2
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(16, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_1)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(16, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_3)
        masked_subblock4 = self.masked_subblock4(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock4)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index7')
        store = ops.store('buf50', get_index_4, where, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf49', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock3(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index5')
        load = ops.load('buf49', get_index)
        return load
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index1')
        load = ops.load('buf49', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(8, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)

class op90_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 6, p1: 12, p2: 64}
    index0 = p2
    index1 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + ((p2))
    index2 = p0 + 6*(ModularIndexing((p2), 1, 8))
    index3 = (p2)
    index4 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + (((p2))) + 8
    index5 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + ((((p2)) - 8))
    index6 = 2304*p0 + 192*p1 + ((p2 - 16)) + 16
    index7 = 64*p0 + 384*p1 + p2
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(16, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_1)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(16, torch.int64)

        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_3)
        masked_subblock4 = self.masked_subblock4(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock4)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index7')
        store = ops.store('buf90', get_index_4, where, None)

        load = ops.load('buf89', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock3(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index5')
        load = ops.load('buf89', get_index)
        return load
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index1')
        load = ops.load('buf89', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(8, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(8, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(16, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock3 = self.masked_subblock3(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock2, masked_subblock3)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index2')
